# 500 Muse Tricks

🌐 [中文版](README.md)

*500 battle-tested tricks to squeeze every drop out of Muse — for all the little chores that make you think "AI should be doing this."*

*🥚 Easter egg: this README was written by Muse itself. Yes — it's writing its own manual.*

![tricks](https://img.shields.io/badge/tricks-500-blue)
![language](https://img.shields.io/badge/English-translation-orange)
![PRs](https://img.shields.io/badge/PRs-welcome-brightgreen)

> These are Muse tricks I actually use every day: making money, reading earnings reports, running accounts, tracking price drops, organizing files…
> Each trick does exactly one thing: **nails your pain**, **hands you three prompts**, and adds one line of **real talk** on which step it can't handle — that part's on you.
>
> 500 entries too many to scroll? They're all collapsible — click to expand. My invite code appears exactly once on this page, never spammed into every entry — you know how it is.

## How to use

No need to read top to bottom. Just Ctrl+F whatever's bugging you, open that entry, and copy the three prompts to Muse.

Muse is Meta's AI personal assistant — with your permission it can read your email, calendar, health data, and bank statements. You talk, it does the work. New here? Use my invite code **VRPX8E** when signing up.

Found it useful? Drop a ⭐ Star and forward it to a friend who's drowning in chores.
